These are his views and examples. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:11:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:12:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:12:30}
- On the slow-responding husband: she complains of him and thinks he is busy with others, and he advises patience and giving up constant criticism that breeds enmity, saying the fault may lie with both: 'you hurry and I hurry, so when shall we agree'. He likens absorbing shocks to the rubber tyres on quaysides that protect ships and calls the episode 'the harbour of safety'. He says [[person/umar-ibn-al-khattab|Umar]] knew Abu Bakr's sharpness at the Saqifa and prepared words to say when he flared, and that Abu Bakr said the same of Umar. See [[event/saqifa|the Saqifa]]. This is the lecturer's account, without a chain. (needs_source: the account of Umar and Abu Bakr at the Saqifa has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:13:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:14:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:14:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:15:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:15:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:16:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:17:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:17:30}
- He advises a wife who raises ten matters at once to discuss one or two, for a home is not a barracks where soldiers take an officer's orders. He says a home ruled by domination is a grave even if it is a palace, and that understanding the other is a serious matter. These are the lecturer's counsels. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:17:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:18:30}
- Calls: a caller from Iraq asks about repeating a prayer and the lecturer replies the episode is on the first year and that jurisprudence has its own programmes; a caller asks for ten minutes of calls on the episode's topic; a caller asks about women's gossip exposing households; a man married a year and a half from Mauritania asks for advice; and a caller from Norway whose sister married a man who went to Sweden and learned five years later that he had divorced her through his father's proxy without telling her. The call details come from garbled text. (needs_source: the text of the calls is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:19:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:24:30}
- He says disclosing household secrets is a disaster, and that one of the greatest betrayals before God is for a man to tell his wife something and she tell her neighbours, or she tells him and he tells his companions (given in sense, with no chain). He says it is forbidden for a man to speak of the secrets between him and his wife, and for a woman to speak of them even to her mother and sister (a ruling the lecturer states). He promises a special episode. See [[concept/kitman-al-sirr|keeping a secret]]. (needs_source: the report on the greatest betrayals has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:27:00}
- Among the calls: a man married five months asks how to treat a woman; a man married a year wants a word for both spouses' parents; and a wife complains that her husband gets angry at her in front of the children if the maid makes a mistake and that she does not welcome him after his journeys. He postpones the answers. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:27:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:29:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:30:00}
- He answers: each spouse should read the other's page in faith without criticism, and hold back from judgments. If the husband is hot-tempered he should tell his wife at the start: when I flare up, avoid me and put off the discussion until the wind drops, 'we close the roads of dialogue in psychological storms'. He says a divorce after twenty or thirty years was seeded in the first year, and that a husband should not carry to his mother and sister what goes on in the home. He wants complete manliness with an embracing femininity. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:31:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:32:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:32:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:33:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:34:00}
- On the man who divorced through his father's proxy without his wife's knowledge for five years: he congratulates the family on being rid of him, since a man who leaves his wife for five years and divorces her without telling her is not trustworthy, saying the fault lies with his family who did not raise him, and advises establishing the divorce in law and in the shari'a so that she can marry. This is the lecturer's opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:34:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:35:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:36:00}
- To the wife whose husband gets angry because the maid erred: he thinks a candidate for marriage should have a psychological check as well as the medical one, and counts behavioural disability as the worst of disabilities, naming no other a disability, since others are a trial. He advises the wife to obey God in him even if he disobeys God in her and to do good to the one who wrongs her, citing pardon and reconciliation ([[passage/quran-42-40|al-Shura 40]]). He closes that the first year is the year of reading the other's page and putting the dots on the letters, without judgments, constant criticism or belittling, for the slow and calm one may be of good character. These are the lecturer's views. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:36:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:37:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:37:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:38:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:38:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:39:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep07 00:39:30}

## Episode 8: the language of dialogue between spouses and the obstacles to affection
- He opens with a parable: an Arab who knows only Arabic sits two hours with a Chinese who knows only Chinese, and what does either understand of the other? He says many spouses speak, each, a language the other does not: reason and emotion, order and disorder, temper and coldness, social intelligence and dullness, humour and criticism. If they sit together forty years, what is the result, and will there be the affection God mentions? {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:00:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:02:30}
- He says one should neither belittle good deeds nor magnify bad ones, that a good deed is to be published and a bad one concealed. He recalls that his senior teachers used to see visitors off at the front door saying: conceal whatever faults you saw at our house, for homes are private, and says everyone has weak spots and gaps, and that a husband is no angel nor a wife. This is the lecturer's personal recollection. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:04:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:04:30}
- He says a study, as he puts it, of newborns shows that in the first two months the baby tests his mother and father, and if left to cry for two or three days and certain there is no way out, falls silent, and that Westerners leave him to cry to widen his lungs while Arabs are emotional. He concludes that spouses should learn from this child not to mock the other's gifts. The study is the lecturer's claim with no source. (needs_source: the newborn study has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:06:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:07:00}
- He says pure rationality harms life because life is not a mathematical equation, that Islam addressed both reason and emotion, and he distinguishes reflection (tafakkur), a thought that is then acted on, from pondering consequences (tadabbur). He asks young men and women to ponder the consequences of marriage so that it is not just a white dress, a wedding and gifts. The first obstacle to affection, to him, is mocking the other's qualities. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:09:30}
- He criticises the double standard: when our daughter marries we want the control in her hands, but when our son marries we tell him to beware of women and be harsh from the start. He says a parent whose son or daughter complains of a spouse must hear both sides, and cites the story of [[person/dawud|Dawud]] with the two disputants, the owner of one ewe and the owner of ninety-nine, and that Dawud realised his error on hearing the other ([[passage/quran-38-23|Sad 23]], [[passage/quran-38-24|24]]). He recalls the instruction that hatred of a people must not lead one to abandon justice ([[passage/quran-5-8|al-Ma'ida 8]]). {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:11:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:12:00}
- The second obstacle: pride, which kills affection, so that not a breath of affection stays in a proud household. He calls for accepting the other and moving to the ground the other stands on, citing a line of [[person/al-mutanabbi|al-Mutanabbi]] on excusing the longing, and notes that [[person/abu-nuwas|Abu Nuwas]] wrote love poetry to his dark-skinned slave-girl, as proof that tastes differ. He says some husbands are proud of their lineage and some of their wife's, then after thirty years say it was not meant to be, and that when patience evaporates, tranquillity, affection and mercy evaporate and reactions become worse than actions. See [[concept/kibr|pride]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:13:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:14:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:14:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:15:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:15:30}
- He speaks of 'keys to the personality': he says the law requires a wife to ask her husband's leave for voluntary fasting (stated in sense), that this opens a door of dialogue and spares the husband silence after marriage, and that when the wife says she will fast tomorrow, Monday or Thursday or the white days, the husband is pleased that his house stands on obedience. He says one turns the personality's key at the right time, and turning it at the wrong time does not open the door, otherwise each becomes like two trains going opposite ways that meet only in a crash. He calls this the jurisprudence of social intelligence. This is the lecturer's opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:17:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:17:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:18:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:19:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:20:00}
- He gives the example of a son who spends seven hours before football and a quarter-hour studying: insulting criticism makes him go 'in one ear and out the other', whereas a good approach, such as waiting until the goal is scored, opens hearts. He advises thanking for the cup and the dish, and says the Prophet used to help in his household's work, mending his sandal and patching his garment, and drank from the place where [[person/aisha|Aisha]] had drunk, out of affection (given in sense). He likens good treatment to a balance saved with the other, drawn on at times of anger. These are the lecturer's examples. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:24:00}
- Calls: a caller from France who vowed to make umra on her first journey without asking her husband's leave and is the mother of two small children, to whom he answers that she should wait until God eases umra with her husband or a [[concept/mahram|mahram]], since God placed no hardship in religion; a caller from al-Ain complaining of her husband's roughness and work, and a caller asking for a prayer. On supplication he says it is the marrow of worship, and that a person should keep his food pure and choose the times when answers come. See [[practice/dua|supplication]]. The call details come from garbled text. (needs_source: the text of the calls is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:27:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:28:30}
- In the answers: to a wife who taunts her husband for not praying and smoking the water-pipe, he says do not taunt him but pray for him and see his generosity, then ask him step by step to keep up prayer, without being a school headmistress. To one whose brothers and sisters treat her coldly, he says cutting kinship is not allowed, and if things get hard, there is beautiful forgiveness, beautiful separation and beautiful patience: I forgive and do not reproach, I withdraw and do not cut off, I am patient and do not complain. On repeated divorces he advises the husband to go to a jurist, and that fatwas on air are hard. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:33:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:34:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:35:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:36:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:36:30}
- Among the last questions: a mother who lost her son and weeps, to whom he says weeping is not forbidden but we say only what is true; a man married to two wives where the first keeps her children from their half-siblings, to whom he says this builds a psychological barrier and rivalry over inheritance and urges lifting barriers; and a bride of one month whose husband has hit her four times, who had insisted on the marriage against her family's refusal, to whom he prays for the husband's guidance and invites the couple to sit with a shaykh or reformer. He closes with a call not to be proud over anyone because of race, descent, colour, money or knowledge, which is not of God's religion. The details come from partly garbled text. (needs_source: part of these questions comes from garbled text) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:37:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:37:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:38:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:40:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:41:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:41:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:42:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:42:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep08 00:43:00}

## Episode 9: living together in kindness
- He says ancient civilisations doubted that a woman is a human being, and that modern ones demean her dignity in fashion and advertising and, in some Western countries, by taking her father's name from her after marriage. He says some Arab and Muslim societies still deny women inheritance, education and capacity to act, though Islam gave her the right to dispose of her own property. These are the lecturer's claims with no source. (needs_source: the historical claims have no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:03:30}
- He divides married life into courtesies in the engagement, then exchanges and disputes, then frictions, then clashes and conflicts when the spirit of religion is absent. He says a husband may taunt his wife for being fat or barren and she him, as if they had not read God's saying that He grants females, males, or both, and makes whom He wills barren ([[passage/quran-42-49|al-Shura 49]], [[passage/quran-42-50|50]]). He warns against an uncle, maternal uncle or grandfather poking in to the raising of the children. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:04:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:06:30}
- He says God said of living together 'live with them in kindness' ([[passage/quran-4-19|al-Nisa 19]]), and that kindness needs no definition: a bright face, a smile, a good word, courtesy. He tells how [[person/safiyya-bint-huyayy|Safiyya]] visited the Prophet while he was in retreat in Ramadan, and he walked with her until she reached her camel, then knelt a knee and she placed her foot on it and mounted (given in sense by the lecturer). He says anyone wishing to apply this today opens the car door for his wife or pulls out her chair. See [[practice/itikaf|i'tikaf]]. (needs_source: the report on Safiyya is given without a chain in the episode) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:09:30}
- He says breaking a woman's heart is a moral fracture, and that one who gave a gift and then took it back during a quarrel has committed a breach of decency, citing in sense the likening of one who takes back his gift to a dog returning to its vomit. He recalls the Prophet's counsel in the farewell pilgrimage to treat women well, and asks every household: do I live with my family in kindness? See [[concept/hiba-fiqh|gifts]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:11:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:12:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:12:30}
- He says what we lack is not knowledge but application, and gives the example of a cat that is startled if it snatches the piece of meat from another because it knows the wrong, so how much more a sensible human. He gives wives the counsel of the wife of Awf ibn Muhallim al-Shaybani: do not disturb his sleep or leave him hungry, for disturbed sleep angers and hunger inflames. He notes that the guardian says at the contract 'I marry you according to the Book of God and the Sunna of His Messenger', so living together kindly is part of that. Given in sense by the lecturer. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:13:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:14:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:14:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:15:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:15:30}
- He says a child wants a quarter of an hour of his father, and that the accusation 'I never wrong and you always do' is illogical: you are not an angel nor she a devil. He says the word 'I' was spoken by [[person/iblis|Iblis]] when he said 'I am better than him', and that Iblis's descendants say I did not fall short, I did. He says a person may not realise God provided for him because of this righteous spouse. He cites the Arabic proverb 'if your brother is hard, be soft', meaning be yielding when the other presses. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:16:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:17:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:17:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:18:30}
- He counts miserliness toward wives, in money, in the kind word, in greeting and in prayer for them, among the things that drive kindness away, saying the most miserly is the one who withholds the greeting, and that a wife who is not greeted nor prayed for lives in a grave. He promises an episode on spending, the working wife and her salary (he asks the questions without yet answering). See [[concept/al-bukhl|miserliness]] and [[concept/nafaqa|maintenance]]. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:19:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:21:30}
- He says every person has a space of good and a space of bad, so we live in the space of good and turn a blind eye to the space of bad in three stages: feigned ignorance, overlooking, then forgiving, until the family's boat reaches the shore of safety. He warns against too much complaining, for a constantly complaining wife wearies her husband even when she is truthful, and says a person complains of people and time to God. These are the lecturer's counsels. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:24:00}
- Calls: a caller from Libya whose parents have been at odds for decades; a caller asking about a husband who swears by divorce over every small and large matter and bars his wife from visiting her family; another whose husband asks about her past; a nurse whose husband drinks wine; a woman married twenty-five years whom her husband has left for three months and who demands concessions; a woman whose husband does not want children; a woman asking, after twenty-five years, whether she may refuse the husband's return after a year's desertion; and a caller asking about test-tube babies. The call details come from garbled text. (needs_source: the text of the calls is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:32:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:35:00}
- He answers: consult them and do not belittle the wife's worth, and 'magnify her and she will magnify you'; on swearing by divorce and barring visits, he says the man must fear God, and barring her from visiting her family cuts what God ordered joined; to the woman whose husband asks about her past he says that at marriage he may ask only about the time of the engagement and what came before is not allowed to him (a ruling the lecturer states); to the one whose husband drinks wine he says be patient and pray for him; to the one asked for concessions he says the believer is neither so dry as to snap nor so soft as to be squeezed; to the one whose husband stops her having children he says she has the right to enjoy motherhood and he may not bar her; and to the one asking about test-tube babies he says it is permitted, God willing, if she trusts the laboratory and doctor (his statement). He urges ongoing charity and prayer for one whose father has died. (needs_source: the rulings on asking about the past and on test-tube babies are the lecturer's own with no named evidence) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:36:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:36:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:37:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:37:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:38:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:38:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:39:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:39:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:40:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:41:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep09 00:41:30}

## Episode 10: dazzlement with the West, disputation and submission
- He says the defeated is dazzled by the victor, that the community reeled when it left the way, and that some of its sons are dazzled by the West, which did its work and feasibility studies well but separated religion from everything and made another religion of love of the world. He says the first disease in the community today is the disease of disputation and quarrelling, and that disputation is a feud that distracts from the emergence of truth. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:03:30}
- He says the truth is clear in God's book and the farmer and the scholar each understand it to his measure, and that in every age the leaders disputed with the prophets as they did with [[person/nuh|Nuh]] ([[passage/quran-11-32|Hud 32]]). He cites the verse on those who dispute about God's signs ([[passage/quran-40-4|Ghafir 4]]) and a report which he says Abu Dawud transmitted, al-Hakim graded sound and al-Albani good, warning against disputing about the Qur'an, and a report in al-Tirmidhi and Ibn Majah condemning seeking knowledge to quarrel. He gives the example of someone who says there is no verse on hijab and is answered with the verses of Surat al-Nur. The attribution and grading of the reports are the lecturer's, and their wording is not typed. (needs_source: the attribution and grading of the reports are the lecturer's own) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:04:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:06:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:07:00}
- He answers those who object that a female inherits half a male's share: he says American inheritance law runs to many volumes while the Qur'an has ten lines that cover every case, that a woman inherits half a man's share in only four cases, more than he does in ten, and the same in thirty, and that an infant girl can inherit more than her grandfather in a case of two daughters, a father and a mother, since he has a short life ahead and she a long one. He compares it to a traffic light. These counts and figures are the lecturer's. See [[concept/mirath|inheritance]] and [[passage/quran-4-11|al-Nisa 11]]. (needs_source: the counts, the size of American law and the case description have no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:11:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:12:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:12:30}
- He says those who speak of women's rights want to free her from her lawful man and hand her to an unlawful one, and notes that fifteen thousand elderly French people died in their flats in summer 2003 and no one knew until the smell, whereas among us there are family ties. He urges admiration only of what benefits, in technology and science. He cites the verse on taking the Prophet as judge ([[passage/quran-4-65|al-Nisa 65]]) and the verse on a matter decreed by God and His Messenger ([[passage/quran-33-36|al-Ahzab 36]]). These are the lecturer's views and his telling of the event. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:13:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:14:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:14:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:15:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:15:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:16:00}
- He says our fathers boarded the Western train but caught only the last carriage, fashion and style, not technology and industry. He gives the example of a farmer who sells a feddan of cotton while his son buys a shirt for that price, and quotes [[person/ibn-qayyim-al-jawziyya|Ibn al-Qayyim]] that if non-Muslims excel in an art and the Muslims send no one to excel in it, all the Muslims are sinful. The examples and the quotation have no source given. (needs_source: the quotation of Ibn al-Qayyim and the example have no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:17:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:17:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:18:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:19:30}
- On consulting parents over marriage: he holds that the girl has the choice but with her father's and mother's counsel, that the family is a ship with a captain, that the West has gone loose in this respect, and that parents consulted ask nothing but choose the best from their experience. He says we are charged with strengthening the immune system, not killing the microbes. He compares companies with the family: an employee does not join without a committee, so one must ask about whoever enters a family. He faults stripping a girl of her father's name after marriage and says a woman is beaten every six minutes in America, and that we do not want our societies like that. The view and the figures are the lecturer's, without a source. (needs_source: the figure on beatings in America has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:19:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:24:30}
- He argues from Abu Bakr hiring Abd Allah ibn Uraqit, a polytheist, as guide for the hijra because he found no Muslim expert in the desert; from the Prophet telling [[person/zayd-ibn-thabit|Zayd ibn Thabit]] to learn the language of the Jews, so he learned Hebrew in seventeen days (the lecturer's telling); and from Ibn Mas'ud sitting in the road when he heard the Prophet say 'sit'; and says a Muslim does not dispute religion as he does not dispute medicine. He says faith is not complete until the Prophet is made judge, and mentions al-Khidr and Musa ([[passage/quran-18-78|al-Kahf 78]]) and that Iblis answered the Commander back. See [[person/al-khidr|al-Khidr]] and [[event/hijra|the hijra]]. (needs_source: the accounts of Zayd, Ibn Mas'ud and Ibn Uraqit are the lecturer's without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:27:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:29:00}
- He clarifies that a Muslim is not barred from asking about his religion, reporting that [[person/al-hasan-al-basri|al-Hasan al-Basri]] once meant to leave Basra for the scarcity of questions, and tells the story of a philosopher on a boat who told the boatman half his life was wasted because he did not know rhetoric, and when it nearly sank the boatman asked whether he could swim and, hearing no, said all his life was wasted. He mentions that [[person/malik-ibn-anas|Malik]] had the man who asked how God rose above the throne beaten, and said the rising is known, the how is unknown, belief in it is required and asking about it an innovation (given in sense). These are tales given without a chain. (needs_source: the tales of al-Hasan, Malik and the philosopher have no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:29:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:30:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:31:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:32:00}
- Calls: a caller from Austria complains of Muslim youths who waste their salaries in gambling; a caller thanks him and mentions someone's abuse; a caller asks about 'the neighbour before the house' and parents' rights; a student asks about the idea that friendship with young men is normal at university; a woman asks about a mother giving her share of an inheritance to two of her children; and a caller asks about misyar marriage. The call details come from garbled text. (needs_source: the text of the calls is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:32:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:32:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:33:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:34:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:36:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:36:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:37:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:37:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:38:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:38:30}
- He answers: to the youths who waste their money in gambling he says they have distorted Islam's image and that the more than thirty million Muslims in Europe should leave a mark, though they have no television channel; on the neighbour he says the house now chooses us, and that the neighbour has three kinds of right (given in sense), telling of a Jew in Kufa who sold his house for double its price because the neighbourhood of Abu Hanifa had a price (a tale with no chain); he urges the students to give polite advice and wishes for separate universities for girls and boys; on a mother giving her inheritance to some children he says it is a gift but fills hearts with resentment, and recalls that the Prophet refused to witness a gift to some of a man's sons and not others (given in sense). See [[person/abu-hanifa|Abu Hanifa]] and [[concept/jiwar|the neighbour]]. (needs_source: the story of the Jew and the neighbour has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:40:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:40:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:41:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:42:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:42:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:43:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:43:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:44:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:44:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:45:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep10 00:45:30}

## Episode 11: grace between the spouses
- He offers a remedy for anger between spouses: take a sheet of paper and write on it only the other's good qualities, not faults, since our faults as humans outnumber our good. He says God treats us with His mercy and shows love to us though He is independent of us and sees everything, and that a 'book of good qualities' lets a person find rest with the other. This is the lecturer's counsel. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:04:00}
- He says that when the other party behaves badly it signals that one party's relation with God is not as it should be, and if a person sets right what is between him and God, God sets right those around him, citing God's setting right of [[person/zakariyya|Zakariya]]'s wife. He returns to the space of grace God mentioned ([[passage/quran-2-237|al-Baqara 237]]). {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:06:30}
- He says that whoever says 'my daughter makes no mistakes' or 'my son makes none' has contradicted the saying that every child of Adam errs (stated in sense), and that both sides must be heard. He tells that the judge [[person/shurayh-al-qadi|Shurayh]] told a man who came with a missing eye to wait until the other came, since he might have put out both the other's eyes, and that [[person/dawud|Dawud]] judged before hearing the other side and then realised his error (the story of the two disputants). The tale of Shurayh has no chain. (needs_source: the tale of Shurayh has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:08:30}
- He distinguishes the smile of the heart from the smile of mockery: the first is charity and the second a sin, and says a child perceives the first like a mirror, and that souls are drawn up in ranks. He says people excel by deeds, not by looks, colour or hair. He advises one who was angry with his wife in the morning to phone her from work and apologise, and that the wife should show affection too, and that guardianship is not harm or wrong. These are the lecturer's counsels. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:11:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:12:00}
- He says a wife must not belittle the father before his children nor a husband the mother, that children respect their mother through their father's respect for her, and recalls the prohibition on mockery between people ([[passage/quran-49-11|al-Hujurat 11]]). He advises the husband not to compare his wife with his mother or sisters: a mother gives without counting and her nature differs from a wife's, and the wife grew up in another setting, and he should not expect her to be like [[person/asma-bint-abi-bakr|Asma bint Abi Bakr]] and her like, though he may wish it; see also [[person/nusayba-bint-kab|Nusayba bint Ka'b]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:13:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:14:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:14:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:15:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:16:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:17:00}
- He lists: a wife is detailed and asks about small matters while a man is general and dislikes that, so speak calmly; neither should set himself up as teacher or headmaster over the other; an Arab does not like anyone to dominate him and is advised only by one who loves him, so the husband should open for his wife a way to a study circle or Qur'an lessons; the eye of love covers faults and the eye of dislike shows them. He holds that gentleness is needed with a woman in the days of menstruation, when she is barred from prayer and fasting and her physical and psychological capacity is unusual. These are the lecturer's views. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:17:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:18:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:19:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:21:30}
- He criticises a husband mocking his wife's brother whom she loves, and tells a wife who asks for jewellery and a car that they are not worth a moment of affection with a generous husband. He holds that the market for psychiatry arose because the wife found no ear to listen to her, so she and the little girl must be listened to, that gentleness solves problems, and that gifts treat the symptom and leave the disease. He then sums up: set right what is between you and your Lord, be fair in contentment and anger, write down the other's good qualities, do not treat the wife as a mother, and do not belittle either before the children, or homes become graves. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:26:30}
- Calls: a woman asks about stock-market shares and eating in Chinese restaurants in America; a woman childless for five years whose husband gets angry when she raises the matter and has dropped treatment; a newly married woman whose divorced husband constantly criticises her; a caller on a wife's salary and the period of pregnancy; a woman in France whose husband denied that her friend visited and joked with her, and who works in a restaurant serving alcohol and pork; another whose in-laws do not accept her; another whose husband will not marry off their daughters; and another whose husband cannot hold a dialogue. The call details come from garbled text. (needs_source: the text of the calls is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:27:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:30:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:32:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:36:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:37:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:38:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:39:00}
- He answers: the stock market is not all forbidden but has lawful and forbidden parts needing detail in a fatwa programme; in the West there are halal shops; to the woman working in a restaurant serving alcohol and pork he says there is no need for that work, God is the provider, and whoever leaves something for God is compensated with better; to the one whose husband is angry at the doctor, she should draw close to his mother and sister and take means, being one who relies on God and not one who merely waits; the wife's salary is a matter of grace, not reproach; to the one whose husband's family does not accept her, be patient, keep in touch and pray for them; to the one whose husband will not marry off the daughters, daughters are the flowers of the home and not a trial, and he recommends calling on people of good; to the one whose husband cannot hold a dialogue, he says he has not seen an Arab who is good at dialogue, we are good at disputation: you are with me, you are a saint; you are against me, you are Iblis. He closes that grace is the key to a successful home, that when it evaporates only justice is left, and that life on bare justice does not stand. These are the lecturer's views. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:39:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:40:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:40:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:41:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:42:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:42:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:43:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:43:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:44:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:44:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:45:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:45:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:46:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:47:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:47:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:48:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep11 00:48:30}

## Episode 12: woman's place in the home and society
- He says God preferred no sex over another and no colour over another, only piety confers merit, but the Muslim Arab looks at woman either with a severe look that sees her as a captive through three exits, from her mother's womb into the world, from her father's house to her husband's, and from her husband's house to the grave, or with a loose one that puts her on adverts, perfume boxes and goods. He says neither is from the Book and the Sunna. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:00:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:03:00}
- He says the Qur'an names woman with many words: wife or woman, female, women, mother, genitrix, sister, daughter, paternal aunt, maternal aunt, and that [[person/maryam|Maryam]] is named thirty-four times (the lecturer's count), and that women reform society when they are righteous. He says a daughter is the flower of the home, especially when she has brothers, as she is her father's friend and mother to her little siblings, and her absence takes away affection, intimacy and the smile. The count is the lecturer's, with no source. (needs_source: the count of Maryam's mentions is the lecturer's) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:04:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:05:30}
- He presents a rebellious daughter whom her father and brothers treat harshly and whose small brother treats her as a servant, and says the Prophet used to stand for his daughter Fatima when she came and kiss her between the eyes (given in sense), wanting a tenderness from the father to be planted in the girl. He tells the suitor not to be rough and frowning, and cites an Arab saying: be with people like a tree, they throw stones at it and it gives them fruit, and that it is the fruit-bearing tree that is pelted. See [[person/fatima|Fatima]]. (needs_source: the report of the Prophet standing for Fatima has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:06:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:09:30}
- He lists three groups: those who hold a woman's voice is nakedness (awra) so she does not go out, those who run loose, and the middle. He says the books of fiqh from the Hanafis to the Zahiris do not say a woman's voice is awra except where there is yielding in speech ([[passage/quran-33-32|al-Ahzab 32]]), and he criticises a man who walks far ahead of his wife, recalling that the Prophet broke his retreat and went out and walked with [[person/safiyya-bint-huyayy|Safiyya]] (in sense). He says the man's tasks are guardianship, spending, care and decision, and that the religion is the middle way. The claim about the fiqh books is the lecturer's without references. See [[concept/awra|awra]]. (needs_source: the claim about the fiqh books has no reference) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:11:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:12:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:12:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:13:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:14:00}
- He tells the story of Aisha's betrothal: [[person/abu-bakr|Abu Bakr]] had promised to marry her to the son of [[person/mutim-ibn-adi|Mut'im ibn Adi]]; when the Prophet mentioned Aisha he went to Mut'im's house, where Mut'im's wife said she feared her son would enter Abu Bakr's religion, so Mut'im withdrew and Abu Bakr was glad. He concludes that a house has a captain, that a man consults his wife and then decides, and that Abu Bakr spoke to the man and not to the woman. See [[person/umm-ruman|Umm Ruman]]. This is the lecturer's telling, without a chain. (needs_source: the story of Mut'im and Abu Bakr has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:14:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:15:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:15:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:16:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:17:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:17:30}
- He says removing woman from life causes the community's backwardness, since she works and goes out daily yet people are surprised when she goes to the mosque, and that women prayed behind the Prophet and asked for a day for themselves, so Monday was set for them, and that the Prophet pointed to a separate door for them (in sense), and that women were present at the pledge of al-Aqaba and in fighting. He then also criticises looseness: a woman dominates and belittles her husband before his children and the family breaks up, and he claims a survey in America and Britain found 98 per cent do not wish for a woman manager. The accounts and figures are the lecturer's, without a source. (needs_source: the survey and the historical accounts have no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:18:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:19:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:24:00}
- In the calls: a woman says her husband's family want her to live in their country, and he answers that a wife lives with her husband wherever he is so the family is complete, and that a scattered family hurts the children; a man asks whether his wife may uncover her face before her mother's husband, and he answers that he is a mahram; an expatriate asks about his mother in another country and is told that if she is content there is no harm and if she needs him he should return; a woman asks about a husband's holidays without his wife and he says shorten the time and take her along as a matter of affection; on hijab he says the majority hold to the face and hands. Another woman asks about a husband who left her after her pregnancy and he appeals to his family, and another call raises a sensitive family matter whose details are not written. The call details come from garbled text. (needs_source: the text of the calls is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:27:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:32:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:36:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:37:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:38:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:39:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:39:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:46:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep12 00:47:00}

## Episode 13: manners of dialogue in the home and what a wife dislikes
- He says homes complain not of too little dialogue but of its bad manners, since we were not raised in the manners of dialogue with elders, juniors and equals, and he starts with the wife: what does she like in her husband and what does she dislike? (an invitation to know what opens dialogue and what closes it). {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:02:30}
- The first thing a wife dislikes is a miser. He says miserliness shrinks feeling and is a tree of hatred, that a generous man is forgiven much, that generosity is of word and stance and not money alone, that the miser has lost reliance and certainty ([[passage/quran-51-22|al-Dhariyat 22]]), that miserliness is not known in the engagement but after living together, and that one asked about a miserly suitor who hides it is a deceiver and a false witness. He says in a woman it may be praiseworthy thrift. See [[concept/al-bukhl|miserliness]]. These are the lecturer's views. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:04:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:06:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:07:00}
- The second is the coward, since the believer is a man of decision who makes istikhara and relies on God. He says the wife presents her demands with intelligence and courtesy. The third is the man who does not look after his cleanliness and appearance, and he mentions that the Prophet ate with three fingers and did not reach for another morsel until he had swallowed the last, and ate from what was in front of him (given in sense). This is the lecturer's view and telling. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:11:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:12:00}
- He urges children to mind their mother: she weeps if her son wrongs her and weeps if he honours her, and he cites the verse on lowering the wing of humility out of mercy ([[passage/quran-17-23|al-Isra 23]], [[passage/quran-17-24|24]]), reports from scholars that one who does not pray for his parents after every prayer has wronged them (the lecturer's attribution), and criticises homes for the old. See [[practice/birr-al-walidayn|kindness to parents]] and [[concept/uquq-al-walidayn|disobedience to parents]]. (needs_source: the scholars' saying on praying for parents is attributed without a name) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:12:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:13:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:14:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:14:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:15:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:15:30}
- Among what a wife dislikes: one who is disobedient to his parents, so he tells the girl to ask about a suitor's kindness to his parents; he urges kissing the mother's head and hand and asking her whether she is pleased with you, and the wife asking her husband the same. He mentions the Prophet's loyalty to Khadija (in sense) and Shaykh al-Ghazali's to his wife. Then the lewd man who spreads secrets and abuses his wife's family; he cites God's setting right of [[person/zakariyya|Zakariya]]'s wife and a saying attributed to [[person/umar-ibn-abd-al-aziz|Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz]], when wolves were reported grazing with sheep: we set right what is between us and our Lord and God set right between wolf and sheep. These are accounts without a chain. (needs_source: the accounts of the Prophet's loyalty and of Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz have no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:17:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:17:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:18:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:19:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:23:00}
- Then the light-minded naive man with no social intelligence who meddles in a secret between his daughter and her mother; the man with no protective jealousy over what is sacred, saying that jealousy is praiseworthy when controlled; and the perpetual frowner, whom he urges to smile. These are the lecturer's views. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:27:00}
- In dispute he says the husband should withdraw in bed alone and the wife should not go to the children's room, and she should not be told to go to her father's house since that spreads secrets; the Arab man erupts like a volcano and then calms like a river, so his mother should not be burdened with what he said; and he warns against divorce in anger by words that are counted and then deceiving the mufti, leading, in his view, to illegitimate children. This is the lecturer's opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:29:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:30:00}
- On beating between spouses: he recounts that men in Medina beat their wives, the women complained to the Mothers of the Believers, and the Prophet said those who do that are not among the best of you (given in sense), that one who beats his wife has taken the Prophet's testimony that he is base, and that the generous honours them while the base humiliates them. He criticises a wife who prays against her husband instead of praying for his guidance, comparing it to the prayer of Quraysh. The report has no chain. (needs_source: the report on beating and the Prophet's saying has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:31:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:32:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:32:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:33:00}
- In the calls and answers: to a wife who discovered her husband's infidelity he says be patient, pray for him and do not expose him; to one whose husband beats her and who prays against him and tells her friends he says the prayer of a wronged woman is answered, but she loses the reward of patience and he fears her talk may be not good; to one whose wife does not pray he says treat her gently and bring her to the gatherings of righteous women; to one whose mother arranged his marriage then asked for divorce he says he should not divorce and should honour his mother, and it would have been better not to marry without his parents' wish; to the caller who spied on her husband he recalls the ban on spying ([[passage/quran-49-12|al-Hujurat 12]]). He does not answer a question on an interest-bearing loan, as it is a legal question. The call details come from garbled text. (needs_source: the text of the calls is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:36:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:37:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:38:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:39:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:40:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:41:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:42:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:43:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:44:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:44:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:45:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:46:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep13 00:47:00}

## Episode 14: difference, dispute and taking oneself to account
- He says difference is a law of God in creation, in temperaments, inclinations, ideas, skin colour, and fingerprints, voice and eye prints, but the disaster is when difference turns into dispute lasting weeks, months and years. He says one should look at what is said and not at who said it, that whim is the cause of household disputes, that dispute between spouses can arise in any year, and that the children's differences lead to complementarity and not conflict. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:04:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:05:30}
- He counts a home in which the husband wishes to be invited somewhere away from it as an unsafe home and a grave even if it is a palace, noting that the world for all its width cannot hold two who hate one another while the eye of a needle holds two who love one another. He then calls each spouse to sit with himself before a mirror and ask: why am I always sure I am right? Infallibility belongs to the Messenger of God alone and every child of Adam errs (stated in sense), so if the fault is the other's each time, he should review himself. He says stupidity is of two kinds, mental and social, and the social is the more dangerous in the home. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:06:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:11:00}
- He then has the husband ask: why do I impose my opinion because guardianship is in my hand, is my opinion a revelation sent down? And the wife: why do I dominate as my mother did? He likens one who judges the other to the convex and concave mirrors of the fun-fair, which do not tell the truth, and urges consulting a sincere friend, a pious scholar, a good mother or aunt, not to reveal a secret but to take advice. He says I will not be reconciled with the other until I am reconciled with myself. These are the lecturer's counsels. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:11:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:12:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:12:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:13:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:14:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:14:30}
- He warns against making it a match in which each side wants to win, and notes that a wife who faults her husband's passivity would not accept his temper or beating from him, so the passive is easier. He tells that a Bedouin came to Baghdad and praised [[person/harun-al-rashid|Harun al-Rashid]] as like a dog, a ram and a bucket, meaning in guarding and giving, and al-Rashid ordered him left alone and kept him six months mixing with the people of Baghdad, after which he brought a poem, 'The eyes of the oryx'; and comments that the husband should develop likewise. See [[person/ali-ibn-al-jahm|Ali ibn al-Jahm]]. This is his telling of the story, without a chain. (needs_source: the story of the Bedouin and al-Rashid has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:15:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:15:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:16:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:17:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:17:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:18:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:19:30}
- He says to one who complains his wife is a nag: barren disputation is to ridicule the other's opinion, and lists kinds of disputation. He notes a woman is not required to be perfect in everything, and that the Prophet made clear that your Lord has a right over you, your self a right and your family a right, so give each his due (stated in sense). He says the man who is left one per cent of the family's attention should be given his due, and that the mother gives the children 98 per cent in rearing. These figures are the lecturer's. (needs_source: the percentages are the lecturer's, without a source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:26:00}
- He says a woman is distressed when compared with her kinswomen, that the Prophet would often mention Khadija and Aisha was jealous and said what she said, and that he replied in a way that preserved loyalty (stated in sense), and that on the day of the conquest he told the Companions to pitch his tent by Khadija's grave (the lecturer's statement). See [[person/khadija|Khadija]] and [[person/aisha|Aisha]]. A report without a chain. (needs_source: the report on Khadija and the tent on the day of the conquest has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:27:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:29:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:30:00}
- In the calls and after: he advises a husband who cannot bear his wife mentioning another man with admiration; warns against parents sending relatives to survey a bride; says the suitor speaks two hours with his fiancee and falls silent after marriage, that he used to keep with the mosque youth in Cairo lists to match boys and girls, and thinks a young man should take the committed girl from the mosque; and says a broken family produces a shaky generation while the world awaits true reformers from inside the upright family. The call details come from garbled text. (needs_source: the text of the calls is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:31:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:32:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:33:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:34:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:35:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:36:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:37:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:38:00}
- He notes a husband may treat marriage as having no expiry date, and asks fathers: would you marry your daughter to a man of your own character? He says the Arab man is a big child pleased by a word, that heavy advice breeds hatred, that in forty years of calling to the faith he found a man dislikes being lectured, and that the community is rich in maleness and poor in manliness. He closes that difference is to be expected but dispute is not wanted, and that partners should put their dispute with courtesy and a sincere intention for God, citing God's setting right of [[person/zakariyya|Zakariya]]'s wife.
