These are the lecturer's views and experience. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:39:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:39:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:40:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:40:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:41:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:41:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:42:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:43:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:43:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:44:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep14 00:44:30}

## Episode 15: trial and the pharmacy of hope
- He speaks about big troubles in the area of trial, saying that trial separates the believer from the one who only claims belief ([[passage/quran-49-14|al-Hujurat 14]]), and quotes [[person/ibn-qayyim-al-jawziyya|Ibn al-Qayyim]], in sense, a verse that when the dust clears it shows whether under you is a horse or a donkey. He lists trials: illness, financial hardship, debt, a wife who does not take to her husband, rebellious children, envy, backbiting and an unjust employer, and says each person in trial sees his calamity as though it blotted out the sun. See [[concept/ibtila|trial]]. (needs_source: the verse attributed to Ibn al-Qayyim is the lecturer's attribution) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:04:00}
- He presents a 'pharmacy of hope' of six medicines: first, that everything is by God's decree, and calamity falls suddenly under God's eye and He is the Merciful; second, that impatience turns back nothing of the decree; third, that what you are in is lighter than what you are not in; fourth, that what remains to you is more than what was taken (faith, Islam, family, neighbours, mosque, lessons); fifth, that every decree has a wisdom, citing [[person/ibn-ata-allah-al-iskandari|Ibn Ata Allah]] in sense that if you are given understanding in the withholding, the withholding becomes the very giving; sixth, that every calamity holds a blessing known only to the strongly faithful. He illustrates with a child crossing the road who takes his father's hand. This arrangement is the lecturer's. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:04:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:06:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:10:30}
- He gives examples of a calamity that was a blessing in itself: [[person/yusuf|Yusuf]] in the well and prison and then raised high ([[passage/quran-12-4|Yusuf 4]]), the mother of [[person/umm-musa|Musa]] ([[passage/quran-28-7|al-Qasas 7]]), [[person/maryam|Maryam]] and the birth of Isa, and the Prophet as an orphan who lost his father before birth and his mother at six and so says only 'O my Lord' (the lecturer's inference). He mentions a woman who threw her child into the fire and the Prophet said God is more merciful to His servants than this one to her child (given in sense). He distinguishes hope from despair: the successful man thinks of the solution and the failed of the problem. These are the lecturer's accounts. (needs_source: the report of the woman who threw her child in the fire is not specified) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:11:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:12:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:12:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:13:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:14:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:14:30}
- He gives the example of a student at Copenhagen University who answered an exam question on measuring a skyscraper's height by offering the barometer to the caretaker, and says he became the only Dane to win the Nobel Prize in physics; of Einstein who is said to have been weak as a boy; and of Edison saying genius is two per cent inspiration and ninety-eight per cent sweat. He says work is needed and the optimist succeeds. These are stories without a source. (needs_source: the stories and attributed sayings have no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:17:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:17:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:18:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:19:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:20:00}
- He says part of trial is not finding humility in prayer or leaving the Qur'an copy for six months, and mentions God's descent in the last third of the night (given in sense), calling to [[practice/qiyam-al-layl|night prayer]]. He criticises one who does not make ablution or pray until his son goes into the operating room and then disappears after recovery, describing him as one who worships God on an edge ([[passage/quran-22-11|al-Hajj 11]]), and says the believer is a worshipper in every state. He advises the troubled to wait for relief as an act of worship. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:24:00}
- He tells of an American professor at Harvard who became Muslim on seeing the smile of his Muslim secretary, who said that a Muslim thanks when good befalls him and is patient when harm befalls him, so in both states he smiles (his telling). He mentions Abraham Lincoln, who failed repeatedly and became president at fifty-two. He calls to living in the present, visiting hospitals to feel the blessing, and relying on God after taking the means, recalls Abu Bakr's words in the cave ([[passage/quran-9-40|al-Tawba 40]]), and says that with hardship comes ease ([[passage/quran-94-5|al-Sharh 5]], [[passage/quran-94-6|6]]) and hardship will never overcome two eases. He uses the example of a baby elephant tied by a rope. These are tales without a source. (needs_source: the story of the American professor and of Lincoln has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:27:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:29:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:30:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:31:00}
- In the calls: a woman asks whether her divorce, her child's death and shop troubles are a trial or God's anger, and he answers that it is written and known to God, who is kinder than her parents, that the child who died may be her forerunner at the Pool (stated in sense), and that what befell her raises her in rank; a man who left Iraq and lost his money is told he lost nothing while his faith remains, and to seek forgiveness often; a woman who aborted a pregnancy years ago and regrets it is told, in his view, to give a tenth of the blood-money and seek forgiveness (his ruling, with no named evidence); a woman with depression is told she keeps up prayer and the Qur'an and should think well; another who suspects magic and possession is told it is psychological tiredness and suggestion, to be countered by the Qur'an. He says, of a caller, that the greatest trial is the loss of the Prophet. The call details come from garbled text. (needs_source: the ruling on a tenth of the blood-money and the answer on possession have no named evidence) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:32:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:32:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:33:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:34:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:37:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:37:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:38:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:43:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:43:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:44:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:44:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:45:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep15 00:46:00}

## Episode 16: raising sons and daughters and rebuilding trust
- He asks where the reform of society should begin, and answers that it starts with sons and daughters in adolescence, middle school and university. He says preachers offer a precious stock, the Book and the Sunna, but it has been mixed with customs and traditions, and religion has been narrowed to a few rak'as and Ramadan fasting whereas the Companions' character in the mosque was the same as in the market and at home. He wishes for a special programme before an audience of young people so as to listen to them, and complains that children take speech in one ear and let it out of the other. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:03:30}
- He says the end of this community is not set right except by what set right its beginning (given in sense), and that the geniuses who left their mark on history, such as Ibn Abbas, Ibn Umar, Ja'far, al-Hasan and al-Husayn, Malik, Abu Hanifa, Ibn Taymiyya and al-Tabari, had mothers who devoted themselves to motherhood, whereas today's woman works twelve hours and is struck by the maleness of work and the children suffer. He recalls that the Prophet called women glass vessels. This is the lecturer's summary and his reasoning. See [[person/al-tabari|al-Tabari]]. (needs_source: the link between the scholars' genius and their mothers' full-time motherhood is the lecturer's inference) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:04:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:06:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:08:00}
- He tells that [[person/jafar-ibn-abi-talib|Ja'far ibn Abi Talib]] returned after seven years in Abyssinia and the Prophet made him commander of the army at Mu'ta, where he lost both hands holding the banner until he was killed, and the Prophet gave the good news that he has two wings in Paradise (in sense). He mentions that his mother [[person/fatima-bint-asad|Fatima bint Asad]] raised the Prophet in her house when his uncle Abu Talib took him in, and that the Prophet lay in her grave praying (given in sense). See [[event/battle-of-mutah|Mu'ta]]. These are the lecturer's accounts without chains. (needs_source: the accounts of Ja'far and of his mother Fatima bint Asad have no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:10:30}
- He recounts giving a lecture at a language school in the Emirates and finding that half the pupils, whose parents are Arab, did not follow his clear Arabic, and says there is a wide gap between us and the coming generation. He gives Nuh calling to his son 'board with us' ([[passage/quran-11-42|Hud 42]], [[passage/quran-11-43|43]]) as an example of youth refusing a father's command, and says fathers turn their relation with sons into commands and mothers with daughters, and that luxury has left the children neither moved by encouragement nor by warning. This is the lecturer's experience and view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:11:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:12:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:12:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:13:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:14:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:14:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:15:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:15:30}
- He says the way is to restore trust by linking young people to their ancestors' history: Usama ibn Zayd led an army at seventeen ([[person/usama-ibn-zayd|Usama]]), Hanzala 'washed by the angels' on his wedding night ([[person/hanzala-ibn-abi-amir|Hanzala]]), Abd Allah ibn Hudhafa al-Sahmi before the Byzantine king, Habib ibn Zayd, Mu'adh and Mu'awwidh at [[event/battle-of-badr|Badr]], Ibn Umar at Uhud at fifteen, and that school books give many pages to Napoleon and four to Khalid ibn al-Walid. He deplores a boy reaching thirty whose mother does not trust him to buy a loaf. These are the lecturer's accounts and figures. (needs_source: the ages, counts and accounts are the lecturer's without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:17:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:17:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:18:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:19:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:22:30}
- He calls for the mother to be a mother and the father a father, for the elder's authority and the standing of the godly scholar and teacher to return, and for lessons in creativity and in jurisprudence in schools and universities, saying worldly and other-worldly knowledge are one knowledge. He calls for the value of time: he says [[person/al-tabari|al-Tabari]] wrote forty pages a day and [[person/ibn-al-jawzi|Ibn al-Jawzi]] used to crush dry biscuits and swallow them with water to save eating time (his telling). See [[concept/time-as-resource|time as a resource]]. These are tales without a chain. (needs_source: the tales of al-Tabari and Ibn al-Jawzi have no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:27:00}
- He shows that one minute holds fifteen recitations of the surah of sincerity, or five of the opening surah, or twenty blessings on the Prophet, or seventy requests for forgiveness, or the formula of power and strength (he gives these as estimates), that the Prophet pointed to that formula as a treasure of Paradise (in sense), and that [[person/ibn-al-jawzi|Ibn al-Jawzi]] advised his son that breaths are treasuries. He says watching a match for an hour or half an hour is permitted but is not life. The counts are the lecturer's estimates. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:29:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:30:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:31:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:32:00}
- He says religion is release and not shackles, that flies gather only on the uncovered dish, and that God is the guide, so a righteous man's son may be an unbeliever and an unbeliever's son righteous (citing Hanzala and Abu Amir, Khalid and al-Walid), and that the Prophet allowed one who reached fifteen to fight. In the calls: a caller from Saudi Arabia; a woman says her father has a girlfriend and she sides with him against her mother, and he tells her she must honour her mother, dislike her father's act and pray for him; a caller from Libya who found her husband's messages to a colleague; he notes that the doctor bears listening to problems for forty years by seeking reward and by putting himself in the problem-owner's place; and for one whose face is harmed by water he allows dry ablution if the doctor attests. He closes that the community's capital is its sons and daughters. The call details come from garbled text. (needs_source: the text of the calls is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:32:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:33:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:34:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:36:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:38:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:38:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:39:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:39:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:40:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:41:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:42:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:43:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:43:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:44:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:46:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:46:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:47:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep16 00:47:30}

## Episode 17: differences between man and woman, differing upbringing, and extravagance in joys and sorrows
- He says most homes go wrong when one spouse is not convinced that the other differs from him because of upbringing in a family with its own characteristics, so ideas, inclinations and ways of solving problems differ, and he cites 'the male is not like the female'. He says a scientific experiment in some advanced country, unnamed, found that a boy cries in the first weeks from anger and a girl from fear. The claim has no source. (needs_source: the alleged experiment has no source or named country) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:03:30}
- He lists differences between man and woman in his view: the man is practical, general and rational and thinks of solutions, while the woman is detailed, sees the small points and needs feelings and consultation; the man withdraws at a problem and the woman sits and takes opinions; the mother knows the infant's need better than the father, citing the Prophet's answer on who most deserves one's company, your mother, then your mother, then your mother, then your father (in sense); the man gives when he feels needed and the woman when she feels cared for. He says that if a woman led an army he would not trust her to keep secrets and that her place lies elsewhere. These are the lecturer's opinions and generalisations. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:04:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:06:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:11:30}
- He lists why a husband may fall silent: his words are not heard, or are mocked, or he is by nature of few words, and warns against the tongue (stated in sense, that people are thrown on their faces into the Fire for the harvests of their tongues), and advises the wife not to belittle her man before his children and family nor deny his worth. He says the man may fall into absolute certainty and the wife turn into a black box that keeps every fault since the engagement, and that the man forgets small tasks while the woman attends to them. He mentions a method psychiatrists ask of a child, to draw the family. These are the lecturer's opinions. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:12:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:12:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:13:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:14:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:14:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:15:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:15:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:16:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:17:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:18:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:19:00}
- He calls for intensive pre-marital courses in the jurisprudence of marriage and family, and says a calm man may be criticised by his wife for his calm, and a wife may want a fierce husband like her father. He describes the two partners growing up in different settings: an orderly house where guests come by appointment and a house with an open door, a house led by the father and one led by the mother, a house spending on luxuries and one on necessities, so trouble comes though each house is excellent. He says 98 per cent of the problems brought to him in forty years were because each does not understand the other, and that treating the symptom without the disease does not help. The figure is the lecturer's. (needs_source: the 98 per cent figure is the lecturer's) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:29:30}
- He says homes are not good or bad but different: an open house like that of Hatim al-Ta'i and a house run by appointments, withdrawn families and mixing ones, a girl who grows up a leader, a firm upbringing and a lax one. He warns of morbid reproach among Arabs, which opens old wounds. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:31:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:32:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:32:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:33:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:34:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:35:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:36:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:36:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:37:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:37:30}
- He criticises Arab extravagance in joys and sorrows: he says that when Ja'far was killed the Prophet said to make food for Ja'far's family as they were taken up with grief (given in sense), whereas the bereaved family now feed the mourners; that Umar put out the mosque lamp at a Companion's death because the oil had become the heirs' right (a tale without a chain); and he speaks of obituary notices costing tens of thousands. He says the dower of Fatima was the armour of [[person/ali-ibn-abi-talib|Ali]], her trousseau simple and her house one of the happiest (in sense). He calls for spending a quarter of what is spent on a wedding and marrying the poor with the rest, for officials to regulate this, and closes with the parable of the people of the ship and a prayer for deliverance. See [[concept/al-israf|extravagance]] and [[person/fatima|Fatima]]. (needs_source: the accounts of Ja'far, Umar and the dower have no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:37:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:38:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:38:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:39:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:39:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:40:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:40:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:41:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:41:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:42:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:42:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:43:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:43:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:44:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep17 00:44:30}

## Episode 18: dazzlement with the West, leadership and civilisation
- He congratulates the people of the Emirates, rulers, citizens and residents, on the fortieth National Day, and says a safe home builds a safe society, then city, state and world, praying that the Emirates be a symbol of the unity of Arabs and Muslims. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:00:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:02:00}
- He divides the world into advanced and backward, and says the community does not advance in class conflict nor when authority hates its people, and that some old and young are dazzled by the West and transfer its idea as it is, citing Khedive Ismail's attempt to make Cairo like Paris in buildings and not in industries. He says the West 'humanised authority' and made the ruler close to his people, and that a safe state is one whose ruler is close to it. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:04:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:06:30}
- He criticises the nuclear bombing of Hiroshima and Nagasaki in August 1945 and calls it 'the West's animality', saying he does not call for hatred of the other but for not being dazzled; he faults young men for lip-fillers and cosmetic surgery (saying 1,200 young men did so in an Arab country, from a magazine he read), and the import of cosmetics that he claims contain forbidden substances. He says we communicate with civilisations and do not clash, taking what suits us. See [[passage/quran-17-70|al-Isra 70]]. These are the lecturer's opinions and figures. (needs_source: the figures and the claims about cosmetics have no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:11:30}
- He says civilisation is the creature standing with the Creator and a person without a Lord is lost, mentioning the call of Yunus in the whale, Yusuf in the well and the mother of Musa (without citing verses). He then tells that Montgomery, when the Germans withdrew from the Egypt-Libya border laying mines, sent a unit of Indian soldiers into the mined field so the mines exploded among them and he wrote that only four of our soldiers were hurt, and asks if that is civilisation. He contrasts this with Muslims honouring human and animal. The story has no source. (needs_source: the story of Montgomery and the Indian soldiers has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:12:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:12:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:13:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:14:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:14:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:15:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:15:30}
- He says good leadership brings out the positives in its people and justice is the basis of rule, that some rulers have turned their countries into prisons, faults Europe for glorifying Alexander and Napoleon, and says we should tell our sons of our great figures. He tells that [[person/abu-bakr|Abu Bakr]], as caliph, saw off the army of [[person/usama-ibn-zayd|Usama ibn Zayd]], aged seventeen, walking beside him, asked his leave to keep [[person/umar-ibn-al-khattab|Umar]] to help him, and that the Prophet was angry when Usama interceded for the Makhzumi woman (given in sense). He urges joining the shrewdness of elders with the resolve of youth. These are accounts without chains. (needs_source: the accounts of Abu Bakr, Usama and the Makhzumi woman have no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:16:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:17:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:17:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:18:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:19:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:21:30}
- He tells that when a man came to the Prophet asking, he asked 'have you anything at home?', sold him a rug and a bowl for two dirhams, told him to buy food with one and an axe with the other, fitted the axe a handle himself, and said go and gather wood and let me not see you for fifteen days, and he returned with money (given in sense). He concludes that it is better to find the poor a trade than to give to him, and that charities should find work for families except the sick and the old. He says people lean to whoever has, citing in sense a saying of [[person/ali-ibn-abi-talib|Ali]]. A report without a chain. (needs_source: the report of the rug, bowl and axe has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:26:30}
- He cites drug 606 for syphilis, tried hundreds of times before, and Khalid ibn al-Walid in a battle who opened the river weir so water came and lifted the ships (the lecturer's telling), and says Arabs and Muslims are good at talk of dying for God's cause but not at living for it. He points to the 15,000 elderly French who died in the heat wave in their homes, and to religion as a true component of civilisation. In the calls: a caller from Fujairah on chivalry and individual responsibility; a woman asks about women going out to work; a caller from Morocco congratulates on elections; he tells that Umar, made judge, came back after a year with no litigant and resigned; and says a conference in France called for French women to return home. The stories and figures have no source. The call details come from garbled text. (needs_source: the stories, figures and call text) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:27:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:29:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:30:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:31:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:32:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:32:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:34:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:35:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:36:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:37:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:37:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:38:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:42:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:43:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:45:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep18 00:46:00}

## Episode 19: the law or customs and traditions
- He says the West invented 'the woman question' two centuries ago, and that we have no such question because a woman is a mother, daughter, wife, sister, maternal and paternal aunt and grandmother; houses that listened to them became either ice or blazing ovens. He says God did not prefer the male to the female any more than the sun to the moon, as they complement each other, that the male is responsible for preparing and receiving, and that guardianship is care and embrace, not a raised voice, beating or humiliation. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:04:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:05:00}
- He says people are caught between the law and customs and traditions, that custom sometimes turns into strong laws and disaster follows, and that male rebellion is met with female rebellion when a mother charges her daughter that her future lies in her certificate. He says the engagement begins with the law: the suitor sees the face and hands and his mother and sisters see her, and that concealment in the engagement and false testimony delay the discovery of facts until too late. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:06:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:09:30}
- He says the Prophet married Fatima to Ali in a day, sold Ali's armour for four hundred dirhams with which their home was furnished with a leather mattress stuffed with fibre and a water-skin, and asked neither for a list of goods nor for a deferred dower (in sense); he criticises the rising dower, lists, deferrals and show, calling it 'madness', and the father who says his son must not touch his sister with a pencil yet haggles over his daughter. He says preachers explain and are not heard, and some prefer a stinking donkey carcass to one who reminds them of God. The report and phrasing are the lecturer's. (needs_source: the report on Fatima's trousseau and no list has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:11:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:12:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:12:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:13:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:14:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:14:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:15:00}
- He says if part of the effort before a wedding were given to making the boy and girl understand the husband's and the wife's rights it would be more useful. He tells that Umm Hakim bint al-Harith went to her husband [[person/ikrima-ibn-abi-jahl|Ikrima ibn Abi Jahl]], whose blood the Prophet had made licit, with a safe-conduct for him, so he became Muslim and was killed at the Yarmuk, and says this is a woman's true work. He mentions [[person/asma-bint-umays|Asma bint Umays]], who married Ja'far, Abu Bakr and Ali and bore [[person/muhammad-ibn-abi-bakr|Muhammad ibn Abi Bakr]] and others, and tells that her sons boasted and she told them she had seen no young man better than Ja'far nor elder better than Abu Bakr (a report without a chain), and he criticises obstructing widows and divorced women from marrying. See [[person/umm-hakim-bint-al-harith|Umm Hakim]]. (needs_source: the accounts of Umm Hakim and Asma bint Umays have no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:17:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:17:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:18:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:19:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:23:30}
- He says people take part of the Book and leave part, and in customs they are extreme in joys and sorrows. He mentions Subay'a al-Aslamiyya, who adorned herself after giving birth and consulted the Prophet, who told her to marry if she wished (given in sense), and calls for spending less on weddings and bringing joy to the poor. He says that his own informal survey of families he knew between 1970 and 2010 found that families whose father left no property stayed affectionate while those with property saw children go to court. He says no preference of male over female, citing the night and the day as complementary ([[passage/quran-3-195|Al Imran 195]]). The survey and accounts are the lecturer's. (needs_source: the lecturer's private survey and the accounts of Subay'a have no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:27:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:29:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:30:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:31:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:32:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:32:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:33:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:34:00}
- In the calls: a woman says women are wronged in the courts where the man is believed and maintenance refused to a working woman, and he appeals to the judges, says he is ready to mediate and that divorce kills a household only in cases that need surgery; a second wife whose husband visits less, to whom he says both options are bitter so he asks God to give her patience; a woman told she is barren, to whom he says this is the dignity of medicine and God's decree may be otherwise; a wife asks about her mother's interference in her deferred dower; a secret second wife complains of lack of fairness and he says the first wife must be told; a wife of a musician asks about hajj and umra; and he refers the question of a woman travelling without a mahram to the fatwa programme. The call details come from garbled text. (needs_source: the text of the calls is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:34:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:35:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:36:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:36:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:37:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:37:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:38:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:38:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:39:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:39:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:40:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:40:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:41:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:42:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:42:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:43:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:44:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:45:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:45:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:46:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:46:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep19 00:47:00}

## Episode 20: where is the defect? burying ideas and opinions
- He says homes do not live apart from events in the Arab street and the world, and prays that God secure Muslim lands and spare their blood. In this episode he wants to look for 'the defect': that it is general in society because the bond with the Book and the Sunna, and with the realities of the lawful and forbidden and of rights and duties, has come undone. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:03:30}
- He sets two pages side by side: the page of the Jahiliyya in which they buried girls alive ([[passage/quran-81-8|al-Takwir 8]], [[passage/quran-81-9|9]]) and the page of today in which we 'bury' ideas, opinions and feelings, saying that burying a dissenting opinion resembles burying girls. He gives the example of an emotionally dry wife who buries her husband's feelings and a purely rational husband who buries his wife's, so the heart hardens. This is the lecturer's analogy. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:04:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:06:30}
- The first defect, to him: confining religion to rites, so that one who prays the dawn prayer thinks himself Abu Bakr and one who attends Friday prayer thinks himself Uthman, though worship is wider. The second is that people keep religion in dealings between creature and creature: the killer, the wrongdoer, the one who eats inheritances, the employer who drains workers, will God have mercy on them? (he states in sense that one who shows no mercy is shown none and that God veils the one who veils a Muslim). He ends by criticising the one who leaves no room for creativity with the stance 'I show you only what I see'. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:11:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:12:00}
- He says tyrants repress peoples and unsafe homes repress feelings, so we are tyrants in our homes while faulting the tyrants; that he studied the history of tyrants over two thousand years and found a wretched childhood behind each (the lecturer's claim); and calls for lifting wrong between ourselves, our selves, wives and daughters so wrong is lifted from peoples. He faults confining religion to the mosque, a month and alms. This is the lecturer's view. (needs_source: the claim of having studied tyrants' history has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:12:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:13:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:14:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:14:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:15:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:15:30}
- He complains that many societies do not consult the resolute, citing a saying attributed to the tribe of Abs, then links the Jahiliyya's dislike of the female to the denial of inheritance to women in many of our societies, saying they have rights like those over them in kindness ([[passage/quran-2-228|al-Baqara 228]]). He says a kind of slavery today is to enslave a woman at work, and recalls the counsel 'gently with the glass vessels'. He counts saying 'my son makes no mistakes' as Jahiliyya zeal. See [[concept/mirath|inheritance]]. These are the lecturer's opinions. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:16:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:17:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:17:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:18:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:19:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:20:30}
- On hijab he says the woman who displays herself is proud before God, compares her to Iblis who rejected the command ([[passage/quran-7-12|al-A'raf 12]]), says the command of hijab in [[passage/quran-33-59|al-Ahzab 59]] is addressed to the Prophet's wives, his daughters and the believing women, so one who refuses it is, in his claim, outside that circle, that she believes part of the Book and rejects part, and that religion passes through reason, but a reason with no whim in it, with the law taking precedence over reason. This is the lecturer's ruling on a disputed question of jurisprudence; see [[concept/hijab|hijab]]. This is the lecturer's opinion and ruling. (needs_source: the lecturer's argument on hijab and his judgment on those who refuse it) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:26:00}
- He calls for praiseworthy protective jealousy, mentions the three times at which children ask leave before entering, and says the wrongdoer and killer was a child left to lie and steal. He says the sincere Muslim asks what he has given to Islam. He then asks for 'believing chivalry' not to flatter the tyrant, and to ask our children their aim in life, mentioning high ambitions such as Mu'awiya as a boy. In the calls: a caller from Fujairah agrees and calls for the wrongdoer to admit fault; and a caller asks how to plant these values in children and about wealthy misers. The call details come from garbled text. (needs_source: the text of the calls is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:27:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:29:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:30:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:31:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:32:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:33:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:34:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:35:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:36:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:36:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep20 00:37:00}

## Episode 21: angelic, devilish, predatory and bestial sins
- He opens with the first ten days of Dhu al-Hijja and the pilgrims heading for Arafa, and says the ailment is the multiplication of sins and knowing the ailment helps with the cure. He asks the viewer for paper and pen to note 'four groups of sins': angelic, devilish, predatory and bestial, and says what lies between creature and Creator God forgives with repentance, while wrongs done to people require returning the rights. See [[practice/hajj|hajj]]. The classification is the lecturer's. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:03:30}
- First the 'angelic' sins: pride, which is refusing advice and thinking oneself the owner of absolute truth, and he tells that [[person/umar-ibn-al-khattab|Umar]] came back as judge after a year with no litigant and that [[person/abu-hanifa|Abu Hanifa]] said of his book that it might be the falsehood with no truth in it (the lecturer's telling); then tyranny, grandeur and coercion, saying dignity is that you harm no one. He applies this to running a home, an institution and a state. These are accounts without chains. (needs_source: the accounts of Umar and Abu Hanifa have no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:06:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:10:00}
- Then the 'devilish' sins: envy ([[concept/hasad|envy]]), saying one should ask God of His bounty and not envy, aggression and cheating, which branches into deceit, scheming and innovation, and rancour; he tells of al-Shafi'i and Ahmad visiting each other in illness (his telling) and of Umar with Mu'adh (no chain). He counts cheating in rearing, trade, speech and neighbours' rights as a society on the lip of a volcano. The classification and the accounts are the lecturer's. (needs_source: the accounts of al-Shafi'i, Ahmad, Umar and Mu'adh have no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:11:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:12:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:12:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:13:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:14:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:14:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:15:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:17:00}
- Then the 'predatory' sins: aggression, anger, bloodshed, daring in wrong and pouncing on the weak. He tells that [[person/abu-jahl|Abu Jahl]] on the night of the hijra feared to terrify the Prophet's daughters (his telling), whereas some people terrify millions. He says corruption has appeared on land and sea through what people's hands have earned ([[passage/quran-30-41|al-Rum 41]]), and recalls al-Khidr's words to Musa about whether there is peace in our land (a report without a source). The classification and the accounts are the lecturer's. (needs_source: the accounts of Abu Jahl and al-Khidr have no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:18:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:19:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:22:30}
- Then the 'bestial' sins: greed and love of money, theft, miserliness and niggardliness, cowardice, panic and impatience, and he tells that [[person/ali-ibn-abi-talib|Ali]] passed a carcass torn by dogs and told his two sons this is the world and these are its seekers (the lecturer's telling), and condemns digging into people's honour and private lives. He sums up that these sins are the cause of withdrawn blessing, earthquakes and crises. The classification and the claims about earthquakes are the lecturer's. (needs_source: the link of sins to earthquakes and the report of Ali have no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:26:30}
- He lists the effects of sins: loss of blessing, weakened turning to God, a deviation of the heart, the removal of favours, rebellion of spouses and children, and falling captive to Satan (citing that God does not change a people's state until they change what is in themselves), and says they fasted from food but did they fast from people's honour. In the calls: a woman in France asks about her husband's sisters living with non-Muslims, and he calls for dialogue with them through an Islamic centre and says the husband's jealousy exists; she asks about praying for a son and he says there is no harm in a servant asking his Lord for what he wants; an Algerian caller working in a hotel said to be forbidden is told to look for other work. The call details come from garbled text. (needs_source: the text of the calls is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:27:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:29:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:30:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:31:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:32:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:33:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:34:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:35:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:36:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:36:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep21 00:37:00}

## Episode 22: keys to the personality between man and woman
- He begins by saying summer parts spouses: men are busy with work all year and women with children and exams, so when summer leisure arrives some fill it with trivia and the rift widens, the cause, to him, being the loss of 'the key to the personality' on each side. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:02:30}
- He says psychologists found the Arab woman speaks about 11,500 words a day and the man 7,500 (given without a source), that the husband tires of his wife's talk and should bear it since this is her nature, she being detailed and he general, and that a man's guardianship is only by embracing, listening and attending. He tells the wife not to belittle her husband's hobby such as a football match, for the man is a big child who needs room. These are the lecturer's generalisations. See [[concept/al-qiwama|guardianship]]. (needs_source: the daily word counts have no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:04:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:06:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:07:30}
- He cites a psychological experiment, he says, in a Russian zoo in which the girl calls the animal gently and gives it a morsel while the boy throws a stone at it, says that at two years one child chooses a doll and the other a pistol, that these are natures created, that a man's will is by nature stronger and a woman is moved and weeps at once, and cites 'the male is not like the female'. These claims have no source. (needs_source: the alleged psychological experiment has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:11:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:12:00}
- He gives the example of a host's carpet: a woman notes its colours and number of knots while a man notices nothing, for the woman is more observant and the man, he says, has a keener sense of smell, and he holds the husband should allow for this. He says a woman is more affected by mockery, so the husband must not mock her in front of his family, hers or his children, and should settle things between the two of them. These are the lecturer's examples. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:13:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:14:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:14:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:15:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:16:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:17:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:18:00}
- Among the 'keys to the personality' he lists: a woman's greater changeability by statistics he cites, her early tenderness as in a girl who plays gently with her baby brother while the boy keeps him away, that handwriting shows personality, that a woman cares more for private details, and that she wants to dominate her husband's heart and not share it, citing what is told of [[person/muawiya-ibn-abi-sufyan|Mu'awiya]] and the hair in the morsel (no chain), and calls not to poke our noses into others' lives ([[passage/quran-17-36|al-Isra 36]]). These are the lecturer's generalisations and statistics with no source. (needs_source: the statistics and the Mu'awiya account have no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:19:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:26:00}
- Calls: a caller from Tunisia whose line dropped; a woman pregnant after IVF following four years of marriage who complains that her mother-in-law has changed; a woman at the start of marriage whose husband says she does not respect him; and a woman whose husband mocks her. The call details come from garbled text and the lecturer's answers are not written. (needs_source: the text of the calls is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:27:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:30:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep22 00:30:30}

## Episode 23: the start of the school year and raising children
- He opens with the programme's return after Ramadan and at the start of the school year, and says the soundness of the home sets right the school, university, administration and the whole society because we are one fabric, that the Muslims' ailment is 'behavioural disabilities' and not bodily ones, and that the believer is distinguished by ordering his priorities. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:03:30}
- He says at the start of the year the father and mother worry about the burden of costs and the child about the joy of uniform, book and peers, but the child sees in his mother's hand not a book but a phone and a mirror, and the parents forgot they belong to the community of 'Read', mentioning Hafsa and her keeping of a copy of the Qur'an (an allusion without detail). He calls for care of the educational system: the premises, curriculum, teacher and learner. This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:06:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:07:30}
- He speaks about the teacher: a human who tires and becomes depressed, with a wife, a landlord and debts, so pupils should honour him (he states in sense that he is not one of us who does not honour our elder and have mercy on our young), and the teacher should be content with his pay and sincere in teaching, and a psychiatrist can reveal an arrogant teacher. He says the Prophet never raised his voice at his daughters or at al-Hasan and al-Husayn (in sense). This is the lecturer's view. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:10:30}
- He says, by a statistic whose source he does not name, that 1,439 Western adolescents attempt suicide weekly and that 1,439 adolescent girls conceive out of wedlock, that our societies are still well by God's grace, that the family there has disintegrated, and calls on anyone who sees a defect in a school to build and change. The statistic has no source or date. (needs_source: the suicide and pregnancy statistic has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:24:00}
- He advises fathers to discuss calmly with children and not give orders, to raise a boy on love and not fear, and to be fair and equal between sons and daughters, noting that [[person/yaqub|Yaqub]] loved [[person/yusuf|Yusuf]] and by preferring him planted envy in his brothers' hearts ([[passage/quran-12-8|Yusuf 8]]), and that favouring one child is a defect. He says a father should embrace his smoking son, befriend him and build him up. See [[concept/hasad|envy]]. These are the lecturer's counsels. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:27:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:29:00}
- He warns a mother against accusing her daughter of lying before her friends, which makes a complex and a time bomb, and says that instead of passing on values parents pass on complexes, and that father and mother should forget the children's slights. This is the lecturer's example. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:29:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:30:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:31:30}

## Episode 23 (continued): adolescence, mistakes in upbringing and the answers
- He asks: did we hear [[person/umar-ibn-al-khattab|Umar]], of the loud voice, raise his voice at his children, or Ali scold al-Hasan and al-Husayn, or Imam Ahmad, al-Shafi'i, Abu Hanifa, Malik or [[person/ibn-taymiyya|Ibn Taymiyya]] scold their children and pupils? He says a raised voice is a sign of three things: a psychological illness needing treatment, a complex, and a swollen ego which he calls 'the Pharaonic I' and 'the Iblisian I', and calls for a psychological check on teachers. See [[person/iblis|Iblis]]. This is the lecturer's argument and interpretation. (needs_source: the claim that these figures never scolded has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:11:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:12:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:12:30}
- He turns to the learner and divides adolescence, by educators, into early from twelve to fifteen, middle to eighteen and late to twenty-one, saying some people have a very late adolescence after fifty or sixty, and criticises an eighty-year-old man marrying a girl of twenty, holding that compatibility ([[concept/kafaa|kafa'a]]) includes age. He describes the angry father as issuing decrees like Security Council resolutions that admit no reply, and says complexes are passed from generation to generation. These are the lecturer's opinions and classification. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:13:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:14:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:14:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:15:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy albuyout-alamena/ep23 00:15:30}
- He gives the example of a boy who comes home from school, throws down his bag and shoes and watches football, whom the mother berates in front of his small brother who wanted to sit by him, belittling him, whereas a sound mother says your brother was waiting for you, sit with him and then put the bag and shoes away, and he says this tells a mother with a defect from one the defect has not yet touched.
